The function encodes these positions in compressed sparse\n//   row (CSR) format.\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   cols:          array for column indexes of elements (size should be = n)\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   n:             number of nonzero elements\n//   dim:           number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid initRandomMatrix(index_t* cols, index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t n, const index_t dim) {\n    index_t nnzAssigned = 0;\n\n    // Figure out the probability that a nonzero should be assigned\n    double prob = static_cast<double>(n) / (static_cast<double>(dim) * static_cast<double>(dim));\n\n    // Seed random number generator\n    srand(8675309);\n\n    // Randomly decide whether entry i,j gets a value, but ensure n values are assigned\n    bool fillRemaining = false;\n    for (index_t i = 0; This balances the load for arbitrary row length\n// distributions while keeping the memory access pattern of each thread\n// contiguous and stable across iterations (important for NUMA locality).\n// Each row is still summed by a single thread in the original order, so the\n// results are bitwise identical to the sequential version.\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid spmvCpu(const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,\n             const double* vec, const index_t dim, double* out,\n             const index_t* rowPartition) {\n#pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        const int tid = omp_get_thread_num();\n        const index_t rowBegin = rowPartition[tid];\n        const index_t rowEnd = rowPartition[tid + 1];\n\n        for (index_t i = rowBegin; i < rowEnd; ++i) {\n            const index_t jBegin = rowDelimiters[i];\n            const index_t jEnd = rowDelimiters[i + 1];\n\n            // Multiple accumulators to hide the FMA latency; the partial sums\n            // are combined in the same order the compiler would use when\n            // unrolling, and reduced left to right below.\n            double t0 = 0.0, t1 = 0.0, t2 = 0.0, t3 = 0.0;\n            index_t j = jBegin;\n            for (; j + 4 <= jEnd; j += 4) {\n                t0 += val[j] * vec[cols[j]];\n                t1 += val[j + 1] * vec[cols[j + 1]];\n                t2 += val[j + 2] * vec[cols[j + 2]];\n                t3 += val[j + 3] * vec[cols[j + 3]];\n            }\n            double t = (t0 + t1) + (t2 + t3);\n            for (; j < jEnd; ++j) {\n                t += val[j] * vec[cols[j]];\n            }\n            out[i] = t;\n        }\n    }\n    (void)dim;\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: computeRowPartition\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Splits the rows of the matrix into numThreads contiguous chunks holding\n//   approximately equal numbers of non-zero elements.\n// ****************************************************************************\nstd::vector<index_t> computeRowPartition(const index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t dim,\n                                         const int numThreads) {\n    std::vector<index_t> partition(static_cast<size_t>(numThreads) + 1);\n    const index_t nnz = rowDelimiters[dim];\n\n    partition[0] = 0;\n    partition[numThreads] = dim;\n    for (int t = 1; t < numThreads; ++t) {\n        const index_t target =\n            static_cast<index_t>((static_cast<uint64_t>(nnz) * t) / numThreads);\n        const index_t* pos = std::lower_bound(rowDelimiters, rowDelimiters + dim + 1, target);\n        index_t row = static_cast<index_t>(pos - rowDelimiters);\n        // Keep the partition monotone even for degenerate matrices\n        if (row < partition[t - 1]) {\n            row = partition[t - 1];\n        }\n        if (row > dim) {\n            row = dim;\n        }\n        partition[t] = row;\n    }\n    return partition;\n}"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1077,"cache_read_input_tokens":26944,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1077},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"ab30db80-4aa6-47b5-ab82-74a5af1a8075","uuid":"ab5bbf25-36af-4326-b125-fa8719cfa901","timestamp":"2026-09-06T23:21:51.112Z","request_id":"req_011Ceo3XzKF4ZobdZffykCXt"}

This balances the load for arbitrary row length\n// distributions while keeping the memory access pattern of each thread\n// contiguous and stable across iterations (important for NUMA locality).\n// Each row is still summed by a single thread in the original order, so the\n// results are bitwise identical to the sequential version.\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid spmvCpu(const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,\n             const double* vec, const index_t dim, double* out,\n             const index_t* rowPartition) {\n#pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        const int tid = omp_get_thread_num();\n        const index_t rowBegin = rowPartition[tid];\n        const index_t rowEnd = rowPartition[tid + 1];\n\n        for (index_t i = rowBegin; Using uninitialized\n// storage lets us perform the first touch from the thread that will later own\n// the data, so pages end up local to their consumer on multi-socket systems.\n// ****************************************************************************\nstruct FreeDeleter {\n    void operator()(void* p) const { std::free(p); }\n};\n\ntemplate <typename T>\nusing aligned_array = std::unique_ptr<T[], FreeDeleter>;\n\ntemplate <typename T>\naligned_array<T> make_aligned_array(const size_t n) {\n    constexpr size_t alignment = 64;\n    const size_t bytes = ((n * sizeof(T) + alignment - 1) / alignment) * alignment;\n    void* p = std::aligned_alloc(alignment, bytes == 0 ? alignment : bytes);\n    if (p == nullptr) {\n        printf(\"Allocation of %zu bytes failed\\n\", bytes);\n        exit(1);\n    }\n    return aligned_array<T>(static_cast<T*>(p));\n}\n\n// Touch every page of [data, data+n) from the threads that will use it, so that\n// the first-touch policy of the OS places the pages on the right NUMA node.\ntemplate <typename T>\nvoid firstTouch(T* data, const size_t n) {\n    constexpr size_t pageElems = 4096 / sizeof(T);\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; i += pageElems) {\n        data[i] = T{};\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: fill\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Initialize array with random values\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   A: pointer to the array to initialize\n//   n: number of elements in the array\n//   maxVal: specifies range of random values [0, maxVal]\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid fill(double* A, const index_t n, const double maxVal) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        A[i] = maxVal * (rand() / (static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX) + 1.0));\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: initRandomMatrix\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Assigns random positions to a given number of elements in a square\n//   matrix. The function encodes these positions in compressed sparse\n//   row (CSR) format.\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   cols:          array for column indexes of elements (size should be = n)\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   n:             number of nonzero elements\n//   dim:           number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid initRandomMatrix(index_t* cols, index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t n, const index_t dim) {\n    index_t nnzAssigned = 0;\n\n    // Figure out the probability that a nonzero should be assigned\n    double prob = static_cast<double>(n) / (static_cast<double>(dim) * static_cast<double>(dim));\n\n    // Seed random number generator\n    srand(8675309);\n\n    // Randomly decide whether entry i,j gets a value, but ensure n values are assigned\n    bool fillRemaining = false;\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        rowDelimiters[i] = nnzAssigned;\n        for (index_t j = 0; j < dim; ++j) {\n            index_t numEntriesLeft = (dim * dim) - ((i * dim) + j);\n            index_t needToAssign = n - nnzAssigned;\n            if (numEntriesLeft <= needToAssign) {\n                fillRemaining = true;\n            }\n            double randVal = static_cast<double>(rand()) / RAND_MAX;\n            if ((nnzAssigned < n && randVal <= prob) || fillRemaining) {\n                // Assign (i,j) a value\n                cols[nnzAssigned] = j;\n                nnzAssigned++;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    // Convention: put the number of non-zeroes at the end of the row delimiters array\n    rowDelimiters[dim] = n;\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: spmvCpu\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Computes sparse matrix-vector multiplication using CSR format\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   val: array holding the non-zero values for the matrix\n//   cols: array of column indices for each element\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   vec: dense vector of size dim to be used for multiplication\n//   dim: number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//   out: output - result from the spmv calculation\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid spmvCpu(const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,\n             const double* vec, const index_t dim, double* out) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        double t = 0.0;\n        for (index_t j = rowDelimiters[i]; j < rowDelimiters[i + 1]; ++j) {\n            const auto col = cols[j];\n            t += val[j] * vec[col];\n        }\n        out[i] = t;\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: verifyResults\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Verifies correctness of results by comparing to reference solution\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   reference: array holding the reference result vector\n//   result: array holding the result vector to verify\n//   size: number of elements per vector\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nbool verifyResults(const double* reference, const double* result, const index_t size) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < size; ++i) {\n        const double ref = reference[i];\n        const double res = result[i];\n        if (std::abs(ref) < 1e-10) {\n            // For very small values, check absolute error\n            if (std::abs(res) > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e\\n\", i, ref, res);\n                return false;\n            }\n        } else {\n            // Check relative error\n            const double relError = std::abs((res - ref) / ref);\n            if (relError > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e (rel error: %.10e)\\n\",\n                       i, ref, res, relError);\n                return false;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of rows/columns in the matrix (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Sparsity: 1 out of N entries is non-zero (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -m <val>     Maximum value for matrix and vector elements (default: 1.0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    index_t numRows = 1024;\n    index_t sparsity = 10;\n    index_t iterations = 10;\n    double maxVal = 1.0;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numRows = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-s\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            sparsity = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-m\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            maxVal = atof(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Calculate number of non-zero elements\n    const index_t nItems = (numRows * numRows) / sparsity;\n\n    printf(\"Sparse Matrix-Vector Multiplication (SpMV) Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Matrix size: %u x %u\\n\", numRows, numRows);\n    printf(\"Sparsity: 1 out of %u entries is non-zero\\n\", sparsity);\n    printf(\"Non-zero elements: %u (%.2f%% sparse)\\n\", \n           nItems, 100.0 * (1.0 - static_cast<double>(nItems) / (numRows * numRows)));\n    printf(\"Iterations: %u\\n\", iterations);\n    printf(\"Max value: %.2f\\n\", maxVal);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n\n    // Allocate and initialize data structures\n    std::vector<double> h_val(nItems);                 // Dense vector\n    std::vector<double> h_out(numRows);                 // Output vector\n\n    printf(\"Initializing data structures...\\n\");\n    fill(h_vec.data(), numRows, maxVal);\n    fill(h_val.data(), nItems, maxVal);\n    initRandomMatrix(h_cols.data(), h_rowDelimiters.data(), nItems, numRows);\n\n    // For validation, compute reference solution\n    std::vector<double> h_reference;\n    if (validate) {\n        printf(\"Computing reference solution...\\n\");\n        h_reference.resize(numRows);\n        spmvCpu(h_val.data(), h_cols.data(), h_rowDelimiters.data(), \n                h_vec.data(), numRows, h_reference.data());\n    }\n\n    // Perform SpMV computation\n    printf(\"Computing SpMV...\\n\");\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    for (index_t iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {\n        spmvCpu(h_val.data(), h_cols.data(), h_rowDelimiters.data(),\n                h_vec.data(), numRows, h_out.data());\n    }\n\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n    \n    // Calculate performance metrics\n    const double gflops = (2.0 * nItems * iterations) / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;\n    const double avgTime = duration.count() / static_cast<double>(iterations);\n    \n    printf(\"Average time per iteration: %.3f ms\\n\", avgTime);\n    printf(\"Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\\n\", gflops);\n    \n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults) {\n        print_results(h_out, \"OutputVector\");\n    }\n\n    // Validation\n    if (validate) {\n        printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n        const bool valid = verifyResults(h_reference.data(), h_out.data(), numRows);\n\n        if (valid) {\n            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return 0;\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":135,"oldLines":19,"newStart":135,"newLines":82,"lines":[" //   out: output - result from the spmv calculation"," //"," // ****************************************************************************","+//   rowPartition: precomputed row ranges, one per thread (size numThreads+1)","+//","+// Rows are distributed statically, but the split points are chosen so that each","+// thread gets (nearly) the same number of non-zeroes rather than the same","+// number of rows. i < rowEnd; ++i) {\n            double t = 0.0;\n            for (index_t j = rowDelimiters[i]; j < rowDelimiters[i + 1]; ++j) {\n                const auto col = cols[j];\n                t += val[j] * vec[col];\n            }\n            out[i] = t;\n        }\n    }\n}","originalFile":"#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <memory>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <omp.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nusing index_t = uint32_t;\n\n// Constants\nconstexpr double MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR = 0.02;\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Raw, uninitialized aligned storage.\n//\n// std::vector zero-initializes on construction, which faults every page in on\n// the NUMA node of the (single) constructing thread. Using uninitialized\n// storage lets us perform the first touch from the thread that will later own\n// the data, so pages end up local to their consumer on multi-socket systems.\n// ****************************************************************************\nstruct FreeDeleter {\n    void operator()(void* p) const { std::free(p); }\n};\n\ntemplate <typename T>\nusing aligned_array = std::unique_ptr<T[], FreeDeleter>;\n\ntemplate <typename T>\naligned_array<T> make_aligned_array(const size_t n) {\n    constexpr size_t alignment = 64;\n    const size_t bytes = ((n * sizeof(T) + alignment - 1) / alignment) * alignment;\n    void* p = std::aligned_alloc(alignment, bytes == 0 ? alignment : bytes);\n    if (p == nullptr) {\n        printf(\"Allocation of %zu bytes failed\\n\", bytes);\n        exit(1);\n    }\n    return aligned_array<T>(static_cast<T*>(p));\n}\n\n// Touch every page of [data, data+n) from the threads that will use it, so that\n// the first-touch policy of the OS places the pages on the right NUMA node.\ntemplate <typename T>\nvoid firstTouch(T* data, const size_t n) {\n    constexpr size_t pageElems = 4096 / sizeof(T);\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; i += pageElems) {\n        data[i] = T{};\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: fill\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Initialize array with random values\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   A: pointer to the array to initialize\n//   n: number of elements in the array\n//   maxVal: specifies range of random values [0, maxVal]\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid fill(double* A, const index_t n, const double maxVal) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        A[i] = maxVal * (rand() / (static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX) + 1.0));\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: initRandomMatrix\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Assigns random positions to a given number of elements in a square\n//   matrix. The function encodes these positions in compressed sparse\n//   row (CSR) format.\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   cols:          array for column indexes of elements (size should be = n)\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   n:             number of nonzero elements\n//   dim:           number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid initRandomMatrix(index_t* cols, index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t n, const index_t dim) {\n    index_t nnzAssigned = 0;\n\n    // Figure out the probability that a nonzero should be assigned\n    double prob = static_cast<double>(n) / (static_cast<double>(dim) * static_cast<double>(dim));\n\n    // Seed random number generator\n    srand(8675309);\n\n    // Randomly decide whether entry i,j gets a value, but ensure n values are assigned\n    bool fillRemaining = false;\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        rowDelimiters[i] = nnzAssigned;\n        for (index_t j = 0; j < dim; ++j) {\n            index_t numEntriesLeft = (dim * dim) - ((i * dim) + j);\n            index_t needToAssign = n - nnzAssigned;\n            if (numEntriesLeft <= needToAssign) {\n                fillRemaining = true;\n            }\n            double randVal = static_cast<double>(rand()) / RAND_MAX;\n            if ((nnzAssigned < n && randVal <= prob) || fillRemaining) {\n                // Assign (i,j) a value\n                cols[nnzAssigned] = j;\n                nnzAssigned++;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    // Convention: put the number of non-zeroes at the end of the row delimiters array\n    rowDelimiters[dim] = n;\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: spmvCpu\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Computes sparse matrix-vector multiplication using CSR format\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   val: array holding the non-zero values for the matrix\n//   cols: array of column indices for each element\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   vec: dense vector of size dim to be used for multiplication\n//   dim: number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//   out: output - result from the spmv calculation\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\n//   rowPartition: precomputed row ranges, one per thread (size numThreads+1)\n//\n// Rows are distributed statically, but the split points are chosen so that each\n// thread gets (nearly) the same number of non-zeroes rather than the same\n// number of rows. This balances the load for arbitrary row length\n// distributions while keeping the memory access pattern of each thread\n// contiguous and stable across iterations (important for NUMA locality).\n// Each row is still summed by a single thread in the original order, so the\n// results are bitwise identical to the sequential version.\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid spmvCpu(const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,\n             const double* vec, const index_t dim, double* out,\n             const index_t* rowPartition) {\n#pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        const int tid = omp_get_thread_num();\n        const index_t rowBegin = rowPartition[tid];\n        const index_t rowEnd = rowPartition[tid + 1];\n\n        for (index_t i = rowBegin; i < rowEnd; ++i) {\n            const index_t jBegin = rowDelimiters[i];\n            const index_t jEnd = rowDelimiters[i + 1];\n\n            // Multiple accumulators to hide the FMA latency; the partial sums\n            // are combined in the same order the compiler would use when\n            // unrolling, and reduced left to right below.\n            double t0 = 0.0, t1 = 0.0, t2 = 0.0, t3 = 0.0;\n            index_t j = jBegin;\n            for (; j + 4 <= jEnd; j += 4) {\n                t0 += val[j] * vec[cols[j]];\n                t1 += val[j + 1] * vec[cols[j + 1]];\n                t2 += val[j + 2] * vec[cols[j + 2]];\n                t3 += val[j + 3] * vec[cols[j + 3]];\n            }\n            double t = (t0 + t1) + (t2 + t3);\n            for (; j < jEnd; ++j) {\n                t += val[j] * vec[cols[j]];\n            }\n            out[i] = t;\n        }\n    }\n    (void)dim;\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: computeRowPartition\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Splits the rows of the matrix into numThreads contiguous chunks holding\n//   approximately equal numbers of non-zero elements.\n// ****************************************************************************\nstd::vector<index_t> computeRowPartition(const index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t dim,\n                                         const int numThreads) {\n    std::vector<index_t> partition(static_cast<size_t>(numThreads) + 1);\n    const index_t nnz = rowDelimiters[dim];\n\n    partition[0] = 0;\n    partition[numThreads] = dim;\n    for (int t = 1; t < numThreads; ++t) {\n        const index_t target =\n            static_cast<index_t>((static_cast<uint64_t>(nnz) * t) / numThreads);\n        const index_t* pos = std::lower_bound(rowDelimiters, rowDelimiters + dim + 1, target);\n        index_t row = static_cast<index_t>(pos - rowDelimiters);\n        // Keep the partition monotone even for degenerate matrices\n        if (row < partition[t - 1]) {\n            row = partition[t - 1];\n        }\n        if (row > dim) {\n            row = dim;\n        }\n        partition[t] = row;\n    }\n    return partition;\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: verifyResults\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Verifies correctness of results by comparing to reference solution\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   reference: array holding the reference result vector\n//   result: array holding the result vector to verify\n//   size: number of elements per vector\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nbool verifyResults(const double* reference, const double* result, const index_t size) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < size; ++i) {\n        const double ref = reference[i];\n        const double res = result[i];\n        if (std::abs(ref) < 1e-10) {\n            // For very small values, check absolute error\n            if (std::abs(res) > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e\\n\", i, ref, res);\n                return false;\n            }\n        } else {\n            // Check relative error\n            const double relError = std::abs((res - ref) / ref);\n            if (relError > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e (rel error: %.10e)\\n\",\n                       i, ref, res, relError);\n                return false;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of rows/columns in the matrix (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Sparsity: 1 out of N entries is non-zero (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -m <val>     Maximum value for matrix and vector elements (default: 1.0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    index_t numRows = 1024;\n    index_t sparsity = 10;\n    index_t iterations = 10;\n    double maxVal = 1.0;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numRows = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-s\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            sparsity = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-m\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            maxVal = atof(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Calculate number of non-zero elements\n    const index_t nItems = (numRows * numRows) / sparsity;\n\n    printf(\"Sparse Matrix-Vector Multiplication (SpMV) Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Matrix size: %u x %u\\n\", numRows, numRows);\n    printf(\"Sparsity: 1 out of %u entries is non-zero\\n\", sparsity);\n    printf(\"Non-zero elements: %u (%.2f%% sparse)\\n\", \n           nItems, 100.0 * (1.0 - static_cast<double>(nItems) / (numRows * numRows)));\n    printf(\"Iterations: %u\\n\", iterations);\n    printf(\"Max value: %.2f\\n\", maxVal);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n\n    // Allocate and initialize data structures\n    std::vector<double> h_val(nItems);                  // Non-zero values\n    std::vector<index_t> h_cols(nItems);                // Column indices\n    std::vector<index_t> h_rowDelimiters(numRows + 1);  // Row delimiters\n    std::vector<double> h_vec(numRows);                 // Dense vector\n    std::vector<double> h_out(numRows);                 // Output vector\n\n    printf(\"Initializing data structures...\\n\");\n    fill(h_vec.data(), numRows, maxVal);\n    fill(h_val.data(), nItems, maxVal);\n    initRandomMatrix(h_cols.data(), h_rowDelimiters.data(), nItems, numRows);\n\n    // For validation, compute reference solution\n    std::vector<double> h_reference;\n    if (validate) {\n        printf(\"Computing reference solution...\\n\");\n        h_reference.resize(numRows);\n        spmvCpu(h_val.data(), h_cols.data(), h_rowDelimiters.data(), \n                h_vec.data(), numRows, h_reference.data());\n    }\n\n    // Perform SpMV computation\n    printf(\"Computing SpMV...\\n\");\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    for (index_t iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {\n        spmvCpu(h_val.data(), h_cols.data(), h_rowDelimiters.data(),\n                h_vec.data(), numRows, h_out.data());\n    }\n\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n    \n    // Calculate performance metrics\n    const double gflops = (2.0 * nItems * iterations) / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;\n    const double avgTime = duration.count() / static_cast<double>(iterations);\n    \n    printf(\"Average time per iteration: %.3f ms\\n\", avgTime);\n    printf(\"Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\\n\", gflops);\n    \n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults) {\n        print_results(h_out, \"OutputVector\");\n    }\n\n    // Validation\n    if (validate) {\n        printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n        const bool valid = verifyResults(h_reference.data(), h_out.data(), numRows);\n\n        if (valid) {\n            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return 0;\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":146,"oldLines":7,"newStart":146,"newLines":7,"lines":[" // results are bitwise identical to the sequential version."," // ****************************************************************************"," void spmvCpu(const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,","-             const double* vec, const index_t dim, double* out,","+             const double* vec, [[maybe_unused]] const index_t dim, double* out,","              const index_t* rowPartition) {"," #pragma omp parallel","     {"]},{"oldStart":155,"oldLines":28,"newStart":155,"newLines":14,"lines":["         const index_t rowEnd = rowPartition[tid + 1];"," ","         for (index_t i = rowBegin; Using uninitialized\n// storage lets us perform the first touch from the thread that will later own\n// the data, so pages end up local to their consumer on multi-socket systems.\n// ****************************************************************************\nstruct FreeDeleter {\n    void operator()(void* p) const { std::free(p); }\n};\n\ntemplate <typename T>\nusing aligned_array = std::unique_ptr<T[], FreeDeleter>;\n\ntemplate <typename T>\naligned_array<T> make_aligned_array(const size_t n) {\n    constexpr size_t alignment = 64;\n    const size_t bytes = ((n * sizeof(T) + alignment - 1) / alignment) * alignment;\n    void* p = std::aligned_alloc(alignment, bytes == 0 ? alignment : bytes);\n    if (p == nullptr) {\n        printf(\"Allocation of %zu bytes failed\\n\", bytes);\n        exit(1);\n    }\n    return aligned_array<T>(static_cast<T*>(p));\n}\n\n// Touch every page of [data, data+n) from the threads that will use it, so that\n// the first-touch policy of the OS places the pages on the right NUMA node.\ntemplate <typename T>\nvoid firstTouch(T* data, const size_t n) {\n    constexpr size_t pageElems = 4096 / sizeof(T);\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; i += pageElems) {\n        data[i] = T{};\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: fill\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Initialize array with random values\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   A: pointer to the array to initialize\n//   n: number of elements in the array\n//   maxVal: specifies range of random values [0, maxVal]\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid fill(double* A, const index_t n, const double maxVal) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        A[i] = maxVal * (rand() / (static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX) + 1.0));\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: initRandomMatrix\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Assigns random positions to a given number of elements in a square\n//   matrix. This balances the load for arbitrary row length\n// distributions while keeping the memory access pattern of each thread\n// contiguous and stable across iterations (important for NUMA locality).\n// Each row is still summed by a single thread in the original order, so the\n// results are bitwise identical to the sequential version.\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid spmvCpu(const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,\n             const double* vec, [[maybe_unused]] const index_t dim, double* out,\n             const index_t* rowPartition) {\n#pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        const int tid = omp_get_thread_num();\n        const index_t rowBegin = rowPartition[tid];\n        const index_t rowEnd = rowPartition[tid + 1];\n\n        for (index_t i = rowBegin; Using uninitialized\n// storage lets us perform the first touch from the thread that will later own\n// the data, so pages end up local to their consumer on multi-socket systems.\n// ****************************************************************************\nstruct FreeDeleter {\n    void operator()(void* p) const { std::free(p); }\n};\n\ntemplate <typename T>\nusing aligned_array = std::unique_ptr<T[], FreeDeleter>;\n\ntemplate <typename T>\naligned_array<T> make_aligned_array(const size_t n) {\n    constexpr size_t alignment = 64;\n    const size_t bytes = ((n * sizeof(T) + alignment - 1) / alignment) * alignment;\n    void* p = std::aligned_alloc(alignment, bytes == 0 ? alignment : bytes);\n    if (p == nullptr) {\n        printf(\"Allocation of %zu bytes failed\\n\", bytes);\n        exit(1);\n    }\n    return aligned_array<T>(static_cast<T*>(p));\n}\n\n// Touch every page of [data, data+n) from the threads that will use it, so that\n// the first-touch policy of the OS places the pages on the right NUMA node.\ntemplate <typename T>\nvoid firstTouch(T* data, const size_t n) {\n    constexpr size_t pageElems = 4096 / sizeof(T);\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; i += pageElems) {\n        data[i] = T{};\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: fill\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Initialize array with random values\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   A: pointer to the array to initialize\n//   n: number of elements in the array\n//   maxVal: specifies range of random values [0, maxVal]\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid fill(double* A, const index_t n, const double maxVal) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        A[i] = maxVal * (rand() / (static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX) + 1.0));\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: initRandomMatrix\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Assigns random positions to a given number of elements in a square\n//   matrix. The function encodes these positions in compressed sparse\n//   row (CSR) format.\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   cols:          array for column indexes of elements (size should be = n)\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   n:             number of nonzero elements\n//   dim:           number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid initRandomMatrix(index_t* cols, index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t n, const index_t dim) {\n    index_t nnzAssigned = 0;\n\n    // Figure out the probability that a nonzero should be assigned\n    double prob = static_cast<double>(n) / (static_cast<double>(dim) * static_cast<double>(dim));\n\n    // Seed random number generator\n    srand(8675309);\n\n    // Randomly decide whether entry i,j gets a value, but ensure n values are assigned\n    bool fillRemaining = false;\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        rowDelimiters[i] = nnzAssigned;\n        for (index_t j = 0; j < dim; ++j) {\n            index_t numEntriesLeft = (dim * dim) - ((i * dim) + j);\n            index_t needToAssign = n - nnzAssigned;\n            if (numEntriesLeft <= needToAssign) {\n                fillRemaining = true;\n            }\n            double randVal = static_cast<double>(rand()) / RAND_MAX;\n            if ((nnzAssigned < n && randVal <= prob) || fillRemaining) {\n                // Assign (i,j) a value\n                cols[nnzAssigned] = j;\n                nnzAssigned++;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    // Convention: put the number of non-zeroes at the end of the row delimiters array\n    rowDelimiters[dim] = n;\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: spmvCpu\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Computes sparse matrix-vector multiplication using CSR format\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   val: array holding the non-zero values for the matrix\n//   cols: array of column indices for each element\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   vec: dense vector of size dim to be used for multiplication\n//   dim: number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//   out: output - result from the spmv calculation\n//   rowPartition: precomputed row ranges, one per thread (size numThreads+1)\n//\n// Rows are distributed statically, but the split points are chosen so that each\n// thread gets (nearly) the same number of non-zeroes rather than the same\n// number of rows. This balances the load for arbitrary row length\n// distributions while keeping the memory access pattern of each thread\n// contiguous and stable across iterations (important for NUMA locality).\n// Each row is still summed by a single thread in the original order, so the\n// results are bitwise identical to the sequential version.\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid spmvCpu(const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,\n             const double* vec, [[maybe_unused]] const index_t dim, double* out,\n             const index_t* rowPartition) {\n#pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        const int tid = omp_get_thread_num();\n        const index_t rowBegin = rowPartition[tid];\n        const index_t rowEnd = rowPartition[tid + 1];\n\n        for (index_t i = rowBegin; i < rowEnd; ++i) {\n            double t = 0.0;\n            for (index_t j = rowDelimiters[i]; j < rowDelimiters[i + 1]; ++j) {\n                const auto col = cols[j];\n                t += val[j] * vec[col];\n            }\n            out[i] = t;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: computeRowPartition\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Splits the rows of the matrix into numThreads contiguous chunks holding\n//   approximately equal numbers of non-zero elements.\n// ****************************************************************************\nstd::vector<index_t> computeRowPartition(const index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t dim,\n                                         const int numThreads) {\n    std::vector<index_t> partition(static_cast<size_t>(numThreads) + 1);\n    const index_t nnz = rowDelimiters[dim];\n\n    partition[0] = 0;\n    partition[numThreads] = dim;\n    for (int t = 1; t < numThreads; ++t) {\n        const index_t target =\n            static_cast<index_t>((static_cast<uint64_t>(nnz) * t) / numThreads);\n        const index_t* pos = std::lower_bound(rowDelimiters, rowDelimiters + dim + 1, target);\n        index_t row = static_cast<index_t>(pos - rowDelimiters);\n        // Keep the partition monotone even for degenerate matrices\n        if (row < partition[t - 1]) {\n            row = partition[t - 1];\n        }\n        if (row > dim) {\n            row = dim;\n        }\n        partition[t] = row;\n    }\n    return partition;\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: verifyResults\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Verifies correctness of results by comparing to reference solution\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   reference: array holding the reference result vector\n//   result: array holding the result vector to verify\n//   size: number of elements per vector\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nbool verifyResults(const double* reference, const double* result, const index_t size) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < size; ++i) {\n        const double ref = reference[i];\n        const double res = result[i];\n        if (std::abs(ref) < 1e-10) {\n            // For very small values, check absolute error\n            if (std::abs(res) > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e\\n\", i, ref, res);\n                return false;\n            }\n        } else {\n            // Check relative error\n            const double relError = std::abs((res - ref) / ref);\n            if (relError > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e (rel error: %.10e)\\n\",\n                       i, ref, res, relError);\n                return false;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of rows/columns in the matrix (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Sparsity: 1 out of N entries is non-zero (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -m <val>     Maximum value for matrix and vector elements (default: 1.0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    index_t numRows = 1024;\n    index_t sparsity = 10;\n    index_t iterations = 10;\n    double maxVal = 1.0;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numRows = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-s\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            sparsity = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-m\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            maxVal = atof(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Calculate number of non-zero elements\n    const index_t nItems = (numRows * numRows) / sparsity;\n\n    printf(\"Sparse Matrix-Vector Multiplication (SpMV) Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Matrix size: %u x %u\\n\", numRows, numRows);\n    printf(\"Sparsity: 1 out of %u entries is non-zero\\n\", sparsity);\n    printf(\"Non-zero elements: %u (%.2f%% sparse)\\n\", \n           nItems, 100.0 * (1.0 - static_cast<double>(nItems) / (numRows * numRows)));\n    printf(\"Iterations: %u\\n\", iterations);\n    printf(\"Max value: %.2f\\n\", maxVal);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n\n    // Allocate and initialize data structures\n    std::vector<double> h_val(nItems); Using uninitialized\n// storage lets us perform the first touch from the thread that will later own\n// the data, so pages end up local to their consumer on multi-socket systems.\n// ****************************************************************************\nstruct FreeDeleter {\n    void operator()(void* p) const { std::free(p); }\n};\n\ntemplate <typename T>\nusing aligned_array = std::unique_ptr<T[], FreeDeleter>;\n\ntemplate <typename T>\naligned_array<T> make_aligned_array(const size_t n) {\n    constexpr size_t alignment = 64;\n    const size_t bytes = ((n * sizeof(T) + alignment - 1) / alignment) * alignment;\n    void* p = std::aligned_alloc(alignment, bytes == 0 ? alignment : bytes);\n    if (p == nullptr) {\n        printf(\"Allocation of %zu bytes failed\\n\", bytes);\n        exit(1);\n    }\n    return aligned_array<T>(static_cast<T*>(p));\n}\n\n// Touch every page of [data, data+n) from the threads that will use it, so that\n// the first-touch policy of the OS places the pages on the right NUMA node.\ntemplate <typename T>\nvoid firstTouch(T* data, const size_t n) {\n    constexpr size_t pageElems = 4096 / sizeof(T);\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; i += pageElems) {\n        data[i] = T{};\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: fill\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Initialize array with random values\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   A: pointer to the array to initialize\n//   n: number of elements in the array\n//   maxVal: specifies range of random values [0, maxVal]\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid fill(double* A, const index_t n, const double maxVal) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        A[i] = maxVal * (rand() / (static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX) + 1.0));\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: initRandomMatrix\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Assigns random positions to a given number of elements in a square\n//   matrix. The function encodes these positions in compressed sparse\n//   row (CSR) format.\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   cols:          array for column indexes of elements (size should be = n)\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   n:             number of nonzero elements\n//   dim:           number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid initRandomMatrix(index_t* cols, index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t n, const index_t dim) {\n    index_t nnzAssigned = 0;\n\n    // Figure out the probability that a nonzero should be assigned\n    double prob = static_cast<double>(n) / (static_cast<double>(dim) * static_cast<double>(dim));\n\n    // Seed random number generator\n    srand(8675309);\n\n    // Randomly decide whether entry i,j gets a value, but ensure n values are assigned\n    bool fillRemaining = false;\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        rowDelimiters[i] = nnzAssigned;\n        for (index_t j = 0; j < dim; ++j) {\n            index_t numEntriesLeft = (dim * dim) - ((i * dim) + j);\n            index_t needToAssign = n - nnzAssigned;\n            if (numEntriesLeft <= needToAssign) {\n                fillRemaining = true;\n            }\n            double randVal = static_cast<double>(rand()) / RAND_MAX;\n            if ((nnzAssigned < n && randVal <= prob) || fillRemaining) {\n                // Assign (i,j) a value\n                cols[nnzAssigned] = j;\n                nnzAssigned++;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    // Convention: put the number of non-zeroes at the end of the row delimiters array\n    rowDelimiters[dim] = n;\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: spmvCpu\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Computes sparse matrix-vector multiplication using CSR format\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   val: array holding the non-zero values for the matrix\n//   cols: array of column indices for each element\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   vec: dense vector of size dim to be used for multiplication\n//   dim: number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//   out: output - result from the spmv calculation\n//   rowPartition: precomputed row ranges, one per thread (size numThreads+1)\n//\n// Rows are distributed statically, but the split points are chosen so that each\n// thread gets (nearly) the same number of non-zeroes rather than the same\n// number of rows. This balances the load for arbitrary row length\n// distributions while keeping the memory access pattern of each thread\n// contiguous and stable across iterations (important for NUMA locality).\n// Each row is still summed by a single thread in the original order, so the\n// results are bitwise identical to the sequential version.\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid spmvCpu(const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,\n             const double* vec, [[maybe_unused]] const index_t dim, double* out,\n             const index_t* rowPartition) {\n#pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        const int tid = omp_get_thread_num();\n        const index_t rowBegin = rowPartition[tid];\n        const index_t rowEnd = rowPartition[tid + 1];\n\n        for (index_t i = rowBegin; i < rowEnd; ++i) {\n            double t = 0.0;\n            for (index_t j = rowDelimiters[i]; j < rowDelimiters[i + 1]; ++j) {\n                const auto col = cols[j];\n                t += val[j] * vec[col];\n            }\n            out[i] = t;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: computeRowPartition\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Splits the rows of the matrix into numThreads contiguous chunks holding\n//   approximately equal numbers of non-zero elements.\n// ****************************************************************************\nstd::vector<index_t> computeRowPartition(const index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t dim,\n                                         const int numThreads) {\n    std::vector<index_t> partition(static_cast<size_t>(numThreads) + 1);\n    const index_t nnz = rowDelimiters[dim];\n\n    partition[0] = 0;\n    partition[numThreads] = dim;\n    for (int t = 1; t < numThreads; ++t) {\n        const index_t target =\n            static_cast<index_t>((static_cast<uint64_t>(nnz) * t) / numThreads);\n        const index_t* pos = std::lower_bound(rowDelimiters, rowDelimiters + dim + 1, target);\n        index_t row = static_cast<index_t>(pos - rowDelimiters);\n        // Keep the partition monotone even for degenerate matrices\n        if (row < partition[t - 1]) {\n            row = partition[t - 1];\n        }\n        if (row > dim) {\n            row = dim;\n        }\n        partition[t] = row;\n    }\n    return partition;\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: verifyResults\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Verifies correctness of results by comparing to reference solution\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   reference: array holding the reference result vector\n//   result: array holding the result vector to verify\n//   size: number of elements per vector\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nbool verifyResults(const double* reference, const double* result, const index_t size) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < size; ++i) {\n        const double ref = reference[i];\n        const double res = result[i];\n        if (std::abs(ref) < 1e-10) {\n            // For very small values, check absolute error\n            if (std::abs(res) > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e\\n\", i, ref, res);\n                return false;\n            }\n        } else {\n            // Check relative error\n            const double relError = std::abs((res - ref) / ref);\n            if (relError > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e (rel error: %.10e)\\n\",\n                       i, ref, res, relError);\n                return false;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of rows/columns in the matrix (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Sparsity: 1 out of N entries is non-zero (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -m <val>     Maximum value for matrix and vector elements (default: 1.0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    index_t numRows = 1024;\n    index_t sparsity = 10;\n    index_t iterations = 10;\n    double maxVal = 1.0;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numRows = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-s\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            sparsity = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-m\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            maxVal = atof(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Calculate number of non-zero elements\n    const index_t nItems = (numRows * numRows) / sparsity;\n\n    printf(\"Sparse Matrix-Vector Multiplication (SpMV) Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Matrix size: %u x %u\\n\", numRows, numRows);\n    printf(\"Sparsity: 1 out of %u entries is non-zero\\n\", sparsity);\n    printf(\"Non-zero elements: %u (%.2f%% sparse)\\n\", \n           nItems, 100.0 * (1.0 - static_cast<double>(nItems) / (numRows * numRows)));\n    printf(\"Iterations: %u\\n\", iterations);\n    printf(\"Max value: %.2f\\n\", maxVal);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n\n    const int numThreads = omp_get_max_threads();\n    printf(\"OpenMP threads: %d\\n\", numThreads);\n\n    // Allocate and initialize data structures\n    auto h_val = make_aligned_array<double>(nItems);              // Non-zero values\n    auto h_cols = make_aligned_array<index_t>(nItems);            // Column indices\n    auto h_rowDelimiters = make_aligned_array<index_t>(numRows + 1);  // Row delimiters\n    auto h_vec = make_aligned_array<double>(numRows);             // Dense vector\n    auto h_out = make_aligned_array<double>(numRows);             // Output vector\n\n    // First touch from the threads that will later own the data. The rows are\n    // split by non-zero count, which for a contiguous CSR layout corresponds to\n    // an (almost) even split of h_val/h_cols, so a plain static distribution\n    // places the pages on the right NUMA node. h_vec is accessed with random\n    // indices by all threads, so spreading it evenly acts like interleaving.\n    firstTouch(h_val.get(), nItems);\n    firstTouch(h_cols.get(), nItems);\n    firstTouch(h_rowDelimiters.get(), static_cast<size_t>(numRows) + 1);\n    firstTouch(h_vec.get(), numRows);\n    firstTouch(h_out.get(), numRows);\n\n    printf(\"Initializing data structures...\\n\");\n    fill(h_vec.get(), numRows, maxVal);\n    fill(h_val.get(), nItems, maxVal);\n    initRandomMatrix(h_cols.get(), h_rowDelimiters.get(), nItems, numRows);\n\n    // Distribute the rows over the threads, balanced by non-zero count\n    const std::vector<index_t> rowPartition =\n        computeRowPartition(h_rowDelimiters.get(), numRows, numThreads);\n\n    // For validation, compute reference solution\n    auto h_reference = make_aligned_array<double>(validate ? numRows : 0);\n    if (validate) {\n        printf(\"Computing reference solution...\\n\");\n        firstTouch(h_reference.get(), numRows);\n        spmvCpu(h_val.get(), h_cols.get(), h_rowDelimiters.get(),\n                h_vec.get(), numRows, h_reference.get(), rowPartition.data());\n    }\n\n    // Perform SpMV computation\n    printf(\"Computing SpMV...\\n\");\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    for (index_t iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {\n        spmvCpu(h_val.data(), h_cols.data(), h_rowDelimiters.data(),\n                h_vec.data(), numRows, h_out.data());\n    }\n\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n    \n    // Calculate performance metrics\n    const double gflops = (2.0 * nItems * iterations) / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;\n    const double avgTime = duration.count() / static_cast<double>(iterations);\n    \n    printf(\"Average time per iteration: %.3f ms\\n\", avgTime);\n    printf(\"Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\\n\", gflops);\n    \n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults) {\n        print_results(h_out, \"OutputVector\");\n    }\n\n    // Validation\n    if (validate) {\n        printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n        const bool valid = verifyResults(h_reference.data(), h_out.data(), numRows);\n\n        if (valid) {\n            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return 0;\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":145,"oldLines":8,"newStart":145,"newLines":8,"lines":[" // ****************************************************************************"," void spmvCpu(const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,","              const double* vec, [[maybe_unused]] const index_t dim, double* out,","-             const index_t* rowPartition) {","-#pragma omp parallel","+             const index_t* rowPartition, const int numThreads) {","+#pragma omp parallel num_threads(numThreads)","     {","         const int tid = omp_get_thread_num();","         const index_t rowBegin = rowPartition[tid];"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}

alignment : bytes);\n    if (p == nullptr) {\n        printf(\"Allocation of %zu bytes failed\\n\", bytes);\n        exit(1);\n    }\n    return aligned_array<T>(static_cast<T*>(p));\n}\n\n// Touch every page of [data, data+n) from the threads that will use it, so that\n// the first-touch policy of the OS places the pages on the right NUMA node.\ntemplate <typename T>\nvoid firstTouch(T* data, const size_t n) {\n    constexpr size_t pageElems = 4096 / sizeof(T);\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; i += pageElems) {\n        data[i] = T{};\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: fill\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Initialize array with random values\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   A: pointer to the array to initialize\n//   n: number of elements in the array\n//   maxVal: specifies range of random values [0, maxVal]\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid fill(double* A, const index_t n, const double maxVal) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        A[i] = maxVal * (rand() / (static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX) + 1.0));\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: initRandomMatrix\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Assigns random positions to a given number of elements in a square\n//   matrix. This balances the load for arbitrary row length\n// distributions while keeping the memory access pattern of each thread\n// contiguous and stable across iterations (important for NUMA locality).\n// Each row is still summed by a single thread in the original order, so the\n// results are bitwise identical to the sequential version.\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid spmvCpu(const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,\n             const double* vec, [[maybe_unused]] const index_t dim, double* out,\n             const index_t* rowPartition, const int numThreads) {\n#pragma omp parallel num_threads(numThreads)\n    {\n        const int tid = omp_get_thread_num();\n        const index_t rowBegin = rowPartition[tid];\n        const index_t rowEnd = rowPartition[tid + 1];\n\n        for (index_t i = rowBegin; Using uninitialized\n// storage lets us perform the first touch from the thread that will later own\n// the data, so pages end up local to their consumer on multi-socket systems.\n// ****************************************************************************\nstruct FreeDeleter {\n    void operator()(void* p) const { std::free(p); }\n};\n\ntemplate <typename T>\nusing aligned_array = std::unique_ptr<T[], FreeDeleter>;\n\ntemplate <typename T>\naligned_array<T> make_aligned_array(const size_t n) {\n    constexpr size_t alignment = 64;\n    const size_t bytes = ((n * sizeof(T) + alignment - 1) / alignment) * alignment;\n    void* p = std::aligned_alloc(alignment, bytes == 0 ? alignment : bytes);\n    if (p == nullptr) {\n        printf(\"Allocation of %zu bytes failed\\n\", bytes);\n        exit(1);\n    }\n    return aligned_array<T>(static_cast<T*>(p));\n}\n\n// Touch every page of [data, data+n) from the threads that will use it, so that\n// the first-touch policy of the OS places the pages on the right NUMA node.\ntemplate <typename T>\nvoid firstTouch(T* data, const size_t n) {\n    constexpr size_t pageElems = 4096 / sizeof(T);\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; i += pageElems) {\n        data[i] = T{};\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: fill\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Initialize array with random values\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   A: pointer to the array to initialize\n//   n: number of elements in the array\n//   maxVal: specifies range of random values [0, maxVal]\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid fill(double* A, const index_t n, const double maxVal) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        A[i] = maxVal * (rand() / (static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX) + 1.0));\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: initRandomMatrix\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Assigns random positions to a given number of elements in a square\n//   matrix. The function encodes these positions in compressed sparse\n//   row (CSR) format.\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   cols:          array for column indexes of elements (size should be = n)\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   n:             number of nonzero elements\n//   dim:           number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid initRandomMatrix(index_t* cols, index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t n, const index_t dim) {\n    index_t nnzAssigned = 0;\n\n    // Figure out the probability that a nonzero should be assigned\n    double prob = static_cast<double>(n) / (static_cast<double>(dim) * static_cast<double>(dim));\n\n    // Seed random number generator\n    srand(8675309);\n\n    // Randomly decide whether entry i,j gets a value, but ensure n values are assigned\n    bool fillRemaining = false;\n    for (index_t i = 0; Using uninitialized\n// storage lets us perform the first touch from the thread that will later own\n// the data, so pages end up local to their consumer on multi-socket systems.\n// ****************************************************************************\nstruct FreeDeleter {\n    void operator()(void* p) const { std::free(p); }\n};\n\ntemplate <typename T>\nusing aligned_array = std::unique_ptr<T[], FreeDeleter>;\n\ntemplate <typename T>\naligned_array<T> make_aligned_array(const size_t n) {\n    constexpr size_t alignment = 64;\n    const size_t bytes = ((n * sizeof(T) + alignment - 1) / alignment) * alignment;\n    void* p = std::aligned_alloc(alignment, bytes == 0 ? Using uninitialized\n// storage lets us perform the first touch from the thread that will later own\n// the data, so pages end up local to their consumer on multi-socket systems.\n// ****************************************************************************\nstruct FreeDeleter {\n    void operator()(void* p) const { std::free(p); }\n};\n\ntemplate <typename T>\nusing aligned_array = std::unique_ptr<T[], FreeDeleter>;\n\ntemplate <typename T>\naligned_array<T> make_aligned_array(const size_t n) {\n    constexpr size_t alignment = 64;\n    const size_t bytes = ((n * sizeof(T) + alignment - 1) / alignment) * alignment;\n    void* p = std::aligned_alloc(alignment, bytes == 0 ? alignment : bytes);\n    if (p == nullptr) {\n        printf(\"Allocation of %zu bytes failed\\n\", bytes);\n        exit(1);\n    }\n    return aligned_array<T>(static_cast<T*>(p));\n}\n\n// Touch every page of [data, data+n) from the threads that will use it, so that\n// the first-touch policy of the OS places the pages on the right NUMA node.\ntemplate <typename T>\nvoid firstTouch(T* data, const size_t n) {\n    constexpr size_t pageElems = 4096 / sizeof(T);\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; i += pageElems) {\n        data[i] = T{};\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: fill\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Initialize array with random values\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   A: pointer to the array to initialize\n//   n: number of elements in the array\n//   maxVal: specifies range of random values [0, maxVal]\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid fill(double* A, const index_t n, const double maxVal) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        A[i] = maxVal * (rand() / (static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX) + 1.0));\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: initRandomMatrix\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Assigns random positions to a given number of elements in a square\n//   matrix. The function encodes these positions in compressed sparse\n//   row (CSR) format.\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   cols:          array for column indexes of elements (size should be = n)\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   n:             number of nonzero elements\n//   dim:           number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid initRandomMatrix(index_t* cols, index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t n, const index_t dim) {\n    index_t nnzAssigned = 0;\n\n    // Figure out the probability that a nonzero should be assigned\n    double prob = static_cast<double>(n) / (static_cast<double>(dim) * static_cast<double>(dim));\n\n    // Seed random number generator\n    srand(8675309);\n\n    // Randomly decide whether entry i,j gets a value, but ensure n values are assigned\n    bool fillRemaining = false;\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        rowDelimiters[i] = nnzAssigned;\n        for (index_t j = 0; j < dim; ++j) {\n            index_t numEntriesLeft = (dim * dim) - ((i * dim) + j);\n            index_t needToAssign = n - nnzAssigned;\n            if (numEntriesLeft <= needToAssign) {\n                fillRemaining = true;\n            }\n            double randVal = static_cast<double>(rand()) / RAND_MAX;\n            if ((nnzAssigned < n && randVal <= prob) || fillRemaining) {\n                // Assign (i,j) a value\n                cols[nnzAssigned] = j;\n                nnzAssigned++;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    // Convention: put the number of non-zeroes at the end of the row delimiters array\n    rowDelimiters[dim] = n;\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: spmvCpu\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Computes sparse matrix-vector multiplication using CSR format\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   val: array holding the non-zero values for the matrix\n//   cols: array of column indices for each element\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   vec: dense vector of size dim to be used for multiplication\n//   dim: number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//   out: output - result from the spmv calculation\n//   rowPartition: precomputed row ranges, one per thread (size numThreads+1)\n//\n// Rows are distributed statically, but the split points are chosen so that each\n// thread gets (nearly) the same number of non-zeroes rather than the same\n// number of rows. This balances the load for arbitrary row length\n// distributions while keeping the memory access pattern of each thread\n// contiguous and stable across iterations (important for NUMA locality).\n// Each row is still summed by a single thread in the original order, so the\n// results are bitwise identical to the sequential version.\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid spmvCpu(const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,\n             const double* vec, [[maybe_unused]] const index_t dim, double* out,\n             const index_t* rowPartition, const int numThreads) {\n#pragma omp parallel num_threads(numThreads)\n    {\n        const int tid = omp_get_thread_num();\n        const index_t rowBegin = rowPartition[tid];\n        const index_t rowEnd = rowPartition[tid + 1];\n\n        for (index_t i = rowBegin; i < rowEnd; ++i) {\n            double t = 0.0;\n            for (index_t j = rowDelimiters[i]; j < rowDelimiters[i + 1]; ++j) {\n                const auto col = cols[j];\n                t += val[j] * vec[col];\n            }\n            out[i] = t;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: computeRowPartition\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Splits the rows of the matrix into numThreads contiguous chunks holding\n//   approximately equal numbers of non-zero elements.\n// ****************************************************************************\nstd::vector<index_t> computeRowPartition(const index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t dim,\n                                         const int numThreads) {\n    std::vector<index_t> partition(static_cast<size_t>(numThreads) + 1);\n    const index_t nnz = rowDelimiters[dim];\n\n    partition[0] = 0;\n    partition[numThreads] = dim;\n    for (int t = 1; t < numThreads; ++t) {\n        const index_t target =\n            static_cast<index_t>((static_cast<uint64_t>(nnz) * t) / numThreads);\n        const index_t* pos = std::lower_bound(rowDelimiters, rowDelimiters + dim + 1, target);\n        index_t row = static_cast<index_t>(pos - rowDelimiters);\n        // Keep the partition monotone even for degenerate matrices\n        if (row < partition[t - 1]) {\n            row = partition[t - 1];\n        }\n        if (row > dim) {\n            row = dim;\n        }\n        partition[t] = row;\n    }\n    return partition;\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: verifyResults\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Verifies correctness of results by comparing to reference solution\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   reference: array holding the reference result vector\n//   result: array holding the result vector to verify\n//   size: number of elements per vector\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nbool verifyResults(const double* reference, const double* result, const index_t size) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < size; ++i) {\n        const double ref = reference[i];\n        const double res = result[i];\n        if (std::abs(ref) < 1e-10) {\n            // For very small values, check absolute error\n            if (std::abs(res) > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e\\n\", i, ref, res);\n                return false;\n            }\n        } else {\n            // Check relative error\n            const double relError = std::abs((res - ref) / ref);\n            if (relError > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e (rel error: %.10e)\\n\",\n                       i, ref, res, relError);\n                return false;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of rows/columns in the matrix (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Sparsity: 1 out of N entries is non-zero (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -m <val>     Maximum value for matrix and vector elements (default: 1.0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    index_t numRows = 1024;\n    index_t sparsity = 10;\n    index_t iterations = 10;\n    double maxVal = 1.0;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1;  // runtime handles binding\n    }\n\n    cpu_set_t allowedMask;\n    CPU_ZERO(&allowedMask);\n    if (sched_getaffinity(0, sizeof(allowedMask), &allowedMask) != 0) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    std::vector<int> cpus;\n    for (int cpu = 0;
